# M7c probe (SR-02 anti-spoofing, ADR 0010 amendment y), hearst, 2026-11-21.
# Probe only: no Skep code change. Proxmox snapshot p7-plus-b61e578 (Andy).
# Andy's one-off approval for this probe: temporary, non-persistent pf and
# ipfw (kldload, enable, rules); nothing in loader.conf, rc.conf or pf.conf.
# hearst: 15.1-RELEASE-p7, Skep b61e578 (unchanged). Test jails m7c-attacker
# (a5d310ee..., 192.0.2.10/24, MAC 02:5e:00:0a:00:10, host end epair0a) and
# m7c-victim (18c5d08e..., 192.0.2.20/24, MAC 02:5e:00:0a:00:20, host end
# epair1a), static NICs on bridge1 (uplink-less, standing approval), made and
# deleted through the API as s7oper (--cacert + --pinnedpubkey, never -k;
# password from the read loader, token never printed).
# Forged MAC 02:5e:00:00:00:01; forged sources 192.0.2.50 (unused, on-subnet)
# and 198.51.100.10 (off-subnet).
# Senders, only inside the attacker jail: rawping (sha256 a88c1997...) and
# rawl2 (new, bpf frame sender, sha256 36d260a7...), both skep_testtasks.
# Evidence: tcpdump -n -e on epair0a (attacker host end), bridge1 (bpf taken
# before the bridge's pfil call, so NOT proof of delivery) and epair1a
# (victim host end: what the bridge delivered). tcpdump timestamps are
# hearst's local time (UTC-5); the harness markers (==== lines) are UTC.
# Deviation, recorded: static jails get devfs ruleset 5 (no bpf). To test
# what a DHCP jail's root can send (ruleset 6001 unhides bpf*), bpf was
# unhidden in the attacker test jail's own /dev only (devfs -m <root>/dev
# rule apply path 'bpf*' unhide); gone with the jail.
# Script mistakes kept in the record: the first control run (02) had no bpf
# in the jail (rawl2 "open /dev/bpf: no such file") and printed sed's exit
# status, not the command's; repeated as 03 after the fixes. In 22 the ipfw
# rules matched nothing (net.link.ether.ipfw was 0, so ipfw had no link pfil
# hook); repeated as 23 with it set. "arp -d 192.0.2.50 pub" in 03 is wrong
# syntax (exit 1); clear_arp removed the entry; fixed afterwards.
# Full logs: ~/skep-test-logs/20261121T203035Z-hearst-m7c-probe-*.log

######## harness/rule file: m7c-tests.sh
#!/bin/sh
# M7c probe test harness (runs on hearst as aba, uses sudo).
# Usage: sh m7c-tests.sh LABEL [test ...]   (default: all tests)
# Evidence per test: tcpdump -n -e on epair0a (attacker host end: what the
# attacker sent), bridge1 (bpf on the bridge, taken BEFORE the bridge's
# pfil call, so not proof of delivery) and epair1a (victim host end: what
# the bridge delivered to the victim). Then the victim's ARP table.
LABEL=$1; shift
JA=skep-a5d310ee-7285-4a23-8185-f6bfcd77d48d   # m7c-attacker, epair0a, 02:5e:00:0a:00:10, 192.0.2.10
JV=skep-18c5d08e-6c1d-480a-b4a4-d03439259bc1   # m7c-victim,   epair1a, 02:5e:00:0a:00:20, 192.0.2.20
AM=02:5e:00:0a:00:10; VM=02:5e:00:0a:00:20; FM=02:5e:00:00:00:01
D=/tmp/m7c-cap; mkdir -p $D

cap_start() {
	for i in epair0a bridge1 epair1a; do
		sudo tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i $i 'not stp' > $D/$1-$i.txt 2>/dev/null &
	done
	sleep 1.5
}
cap_stop() {
	sleep 2
	sudo pkill -f "tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i" ; sleep 0.5
	for i in epair0a bridge1 epair1a; do
		n=$(grep -c '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt)
		echo "  -- $i: $n frame(s)"; grep '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt | sed 's/^/     /'
	done
}
clear_arp() {
	sudo jexec $JA arp -d -a >/dev/null 2>&1; sudo jexec $JV arp -d -a >/dev/null 2>&1
}
varp() { echo "  victim arp:"; sudo jexec $JV arp -an | sed 's/^/     /'; }
run() { echo "  \$ $*"; "$@" > $D/out 2>&1; rc=$?; sed 's/^/     /' $D/out; echo "     (exit $rc)"; }

t_D1() { clear_arp; cap_start D1; run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.20; cap_stop D1; varp; }
t_D2() { clear_arp; cap_start D2; run sudo jexec $JV ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.10; cap_stop D2; varp; }
t_A1() { cap_start A1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -eth-src $FM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop A1; }
t_A2() { clear_arp; cap_start A2
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 ether $FM
	run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.20
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 ether $AM
	cap_stop A2; varp; sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 | grep -w ether | sed 's/^/  attacker now:/'; clear_arp; }
t_B1() { clear_arp; cap_start B1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawping -src 192.0.2.50 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop B1; }
t_B2() { cap_start B2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawping -src 198.51.100.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop B2; }
t_C1() { clear_arp; cap_start C1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op reply -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -sha $AM -spa 192.0.2.50 -tha $VM -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C1; varp; }
t_C2() { clear_arp; sudo jexec $JV ping -c 1 -t 2 192.0.2.10 >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "  victim arp before:"; sudo jexec $JV arp -an | sed 's/^/     /'
	cap_start C2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op reply -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -sha $FM -spa 192.0.2.10 -tha $VM -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C2; varp; clear_arp; }
t_C3() { clear_arp; cap_start C3
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.50/32 alias
	run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 -S 192.0.2.50 192.0.2.20
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.50 -alias
	cap_stop C3; varp; sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 | grep -w inet | sed 's/^/  attacker now:/'; clear_arp; }
t_C4() { clear_arp; cap_start C4; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op request -eth-src $AM -sha $AM -spa 192.0.2.20 -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C4; }
t_C5() { clear_arp; cap_start C5
	run sudo jexec $JA arp -s 192.0.2.50 $FM pub
	run sudo jexec $JV ping -c 1 -t 2 192.0.2.50
	run sudo jexec $JA arp -d 192.0.2.50
	cap_stop C5; varp; clear_arp; }
t_E1() { cap_start E1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode dhcp -eth-src $AM -count 2; cap_stop E1; }
t_E2() { cap_start E2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode dhcp -eth-src $FM -count 2; cap_stop E2; }
t_F1() { cap_start F1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode ipv6 -eth-src $AM -count 2; cap_stop F1; }

ALL="D1 D2 A1 A2 B1 B2 C1 C2 C3 C4 C5 E1 E2 F1"
[ $# -eq 0 ] && set -- $ALL
echo "######## tests [$LABEL] $(date -u +%FT%TZ): $*"
for t in "$@"; do
	echo "==== $LABEL $t $(date -u +%T)"
	t_$t
done
echo "######## end [$LABEL] $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"

######## harness/rule file: pf-ether-static.conf
ether block in quick on epair0a from ! 02:5e:00:0a:00:10 label "A-mac"
ether block in quick on epair0a proto 0x86dd label "F-ipv6"
ether pass in quick on epair0a proto 0x0806 label "C-arp-unchecked"
ether pass in quick on epair0a proto 0x0800 l3 from 192.0.2.10 label "B-ip-ok"
ether block in quick on epair0a label "rest"

######## harness/rule file: pf-ether-out.conf
ether block out quick on epair1a from 02:5e:00:00:00:01 label "out-forged-mac"
ether block out quick on epair1a proto 0x86dd label "out-ipv6"
ether block out quick on epair1a proto 0x0800 l3 from ! 192.0.2.10 label "out-neg-l3"
ether pass out on epair1a label "out-seen"
ether pass out on epair0a label "out-to-atk-seen"

######## harness/rule file: pf-l3-static.conf
block in quick on epair0a inet from ! 192.0.2.10 label "B-ip"
block in quick on epair0a inet6 label "F-ipv6"
pass in quick on epair0a no state label "rest"

######## harness/rule file: pf-l3-dhcp.conf
block in quick on epair0a inet6 label "F-ipv6"
pass in quick on epair0a no state label "rest"
